// Generated code uses the deprecated declarations it makes, such as a stream of a deprecated
// structure. A use in a program still warns.
#if defined(__GNUC__)
#pragma GCC diagnostic push
#pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wdeprecated-declarations"
#elif defined(_MSC_VER)
#pragma warning(push)
#pragma warning(disable : 4996)
#endif

namespace krpc {
namespace services {

class TestService : public ::krpc::RemoteService {
 public:
  explicit TestService(const ::krpc::Connection& connection);

  // Class forward declarations
  class Bound_;
  class ByteString;
  class ClassBase;
  class Client;
  class ClientService;
  class Connection;
  class DeprecatedClass;
  class DocEnum;
  class Encoder;
  class Event;
  class Exception;
  class ExtensionMethods;
  class IConnection;
  class IEquatable;
  class List;
  class NamedTuple;
  class Names;
  class None;
  class Object;
  class Optional;
  class Override;
  class RPCException;
  class RemoteObject;
  class Serializable;
  class Server;
  class Service;
  class Stream;
  class String;
  class TestClass;
  class TestServiceConnection;
  class Tuple;
  class TypeSpecs;
  class UInt64;

  // Structure forward declarations
  struct DeprecatedStruct;
  struct NamesStruct;
  struct PartlyDeprecatedStruct;
  struct TestFlagsStruct;
  struct TestNestedNullableStruct;
  struct TestStruct;
  struct TestNestedStruct;
  struct TestNullableStruct;

  class CustomException : public ::krpc::ServerError {
   public:
    inline explicit CustomException(const ::std::string& msg) : ::krpc::ServerError(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * Deprecated exception documentation string.
   *
   * \deprecated Use CustomException instead.
   */
  class [[deprecated("Use CustomException instead.")]] DeprecatedException : public ::krpc::ServerError {
   public:
    inline explicit DeprecatedException(const ::std::string& msg) : ::krpc::ServerError(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * An exception named RuntimeError.
   */
  class RuntimeError : public ::krpc::ServerError {
   public:
    inline explicit RuntimeError(const ::std::string& msg) : ::krpc::ServerError(msg) {}
  };

  /**
   * Deprecated enum documentation string.
   *
   * \deprecated Use TestEnum instead.
   */
  enum struct [[deprecated("Use TestEnum instead.")]] DeprecatedEnum {
    /**
     * Deprecated enum ValueA documentation string.
     *
     * \deprecated Use TestEnum instead.
     */
    value_a [[deprecated("Use TestEnum instead.")]] = 0,
    /**
     * Deprecated enum ValueB documentation string.
     *
     * \deprecated Use DeprecatedEnum::value_a instead.
     */
    value_b [[deprecated("Use DeprecatedEnum::value_a instead.")]] = 1
  };

  /**
   * An enumeration whose values are named after names in the generated code.
   */
  enum struct NamesEnum {
    /**
     * A value named after the method of a Python class that Enum reserves.
     */
    mro = 0,
    /**
     * A value named after a member of a JavaScript object.
     */
    to_string = 1
  };

  /**
   * Partly deprecated enum documentation string.
   */
  enum struct PartlyDeprecatedEnum {
    /**
     * Partly deprecated enum ValueA documentation string.
     */
    value_a = 0,
    /**
     * Partly deprecated enum ValueB documentation string.
     *
     * \deprecated Use PartlyDeprecatedEnum::value_a instead.
     */
    value_b [[deprecated("Use PartlyDeprecatedEnum::value_a instead.")]] = 1
  };

  /**
   * Enum documentation string.
   */
  enum struct TestEnum {
    /**
     * Enum ValueA documentation string.
     */
    value_a = 0,
    /**
     * Enum ValueB documentation string.
     */
    value_b = 1,
    /**
     * Enum ValueC documentation string, in m/s², equal to
     * ½ ρ v².
     */
    value_c = 2
  };

  /**
   * Flags documentation string.
   */
  enum struct TestFlags {
    /**
     * Flags None documentation string.
     */
    none = 0,
    /**
     * Flags ValueA documentation string.
     */
    value_a = 1,
    /**
     * Flags ValueB documentation string.
     */
    value_b = 2,
    /**
     * Flags ValueC documentation string.
     */
    value_c = 4,
    /**
     * Flags ValueAB documentation string.
     */
    value_ab = 3
  };

  friend constexpr TestFlags operator|(TestFlags a, TestFlags b) {
    return static_cast<TestFlags>(static_cast<int32_t>(a) | static_cast<int32_t>(b));
  }

  friend constexpr TestFlags operator&(TestFlags a, TestFlags b) {
    return static_cast<TestFlags>(static_cast<int32_t>(a) & static_cast<int32_t>(b));
  }

  friend constexpr TestFlags operator^(TestFlags a, TestFlags b) {
    return static_cast<TestFlags>(static_cast<int32_t>(a) ^ static_cast<int32_t>(b));
  }

  // The complement within the values the enumeration declares
  friend constexpr TestFlags operator~(TestFlags a) {
    return static_cast<TestFlags>(~static_cast<int32_t>(a) & 7);
  }

  friend TestFlags& operator|=(TestFlags& a, TestFlags b) {
    return a = a | b;
  }

  friend TestFlags& operator&=(TestFlags& a, TestFlags b) {
    return a = a & b;
  }

  friend TestFlags& operator^=(TestFlags& a, TestFlags b) {
    return a = a ^ b;
  }

  ::std::string add_multiple_values(float x, ::std::int32_t y, ::std::int64_t z);

  ::std::map<::std::string, ::krpc::services::TestService::TestClass> add_to_object_dictionary(::std::map<::std::string, ::krpc::services::TestService::TestClass> d, ::std::string key);

  ::std::vector<::krpc::services::TestService::TestClass> add_to_object_list(::std::vector<::krpc::services::TestService::TestClass> l, ::std::string value);

  ::std::set<::krpc::services::TestService::TestClass> add_to_object_set(::std::set<::krpc::services::TestService::TestClass> h, ::std::string value);

  ::std::int32_t blocking_procedure(::std::int32_t n, ::std::int32_t sum);

  ::std::string bool_to_string(bool value);

  ::std::string bytes_default(::std::string x);

  ::std::string bytes_to_hex_string(::std::string value);

  ::std::int32_t counter(::std::string id, ::std::int32_t divisor);

  /**
   * Returns a struct whose IntField counts the number of times it has been called,
   * so that a stream on it changes on every update.
   */
  ::krpc::services::TestService::TestStruct counter_struct(::std::string id);

  [[deprecated("Use create_test_object instead.")]]
  ::krpc::services::TestService::DeprecatedClass create_deprecated_class();

  ::std::string dump_expression_tree(::krpc::services::KRPC::Expression expression);

  /**
   * Deprecated struct documentation string.
   *
   * \deprecated Use TestStruct instead.
   */
  struct [[deprecated("Use TestStruct instead.")]] DeprecatedStruct {
    /**
     * Deprecated struct Value documentation string.
     *
     * \deprecated Use TestStruct instead.
     */
    ::std::int32_t value [[deprecated("Use TestStruct instead.")]];
    /**
     * Deprecated struct OldValue documentation string.
     *
     * \deprecated Use DeprecatedStruct::value instead.
     */
    ::std::int32_t old_value [[deprecated("Use DeprecatedStruct::value instead.")]];

    DeprecatedStruct() : value(), old_value() {}

    DeprecatedStruct(::std::int32_t value, ::std::int32_t old_value) :
value(value), old_value(old_value) {}

    bool operator==(const DeprecatedStruct& other) const {
      return this->value == other.value && this->old_value == other.old_value;
    }

    bool operator!=(const DeprecatedStruct& other) const {
      return !(*this == other);
    }

    // Ordered by the fields in turn, which is how a tuple of the same values orders
    bool operator<(const DeprecatedStruct& other) const {
      if (this->value < other.value)
        return true;
      if (other.value < this->value)
        return false;
      if (this->old_value < other.old_value)
        return true;
      return false;
    }

    bool operator>(const DeprecatedStruct& other) const {
      return other < *this;
    }

    bool operator<=(const DeprecatedStruct& other) const {
      return !(other < *this);
    }

    bool operator>=(const DeprecatedStruct& other) const {
      return !(*this < other);
    }
  };

  /**
   * A structure whose fields are named after names in the generated code.
   */
  struct NamesStruct {
    /**
     * A value.
     */
    ::std::int32_t other;
    /**
     * A value.
     */
    ::std::int32_t result;
    /**
     * A value.
     */
    ::std::int32_t java;
    /**
     * A value.
     */
    ::std::int32_t krpc;
    /**
     * A value.
     */
    ::std::int32_t class_;
    /**
     * A value named after the class of static methods bound to a connection.
     */
    ::std::int32_t bound;

    NamesStruct() : other(), result(), java(), krpc(), class_(), bound() {}

    NamesStruct(::std::int32_t other, ::std::int32_t result, ::std::int32_t java, ::std::int32_t krpc, ::std::int32_t class_, ::std::int32_t bound) :
other(other), result(result), java(java), krpc(krpc), class_(class_), bound(bound) {}

    bool operator==(const NamesStruct& other) const {
      return this->other == other.other && this->result == other.result && this->java == other.java && this->krpc == other.krpc && this->class_ == other.class_ && this->bound == other.bound;
    }

    bool operator!=(const NamesStruct& other) const {
      return !(*this == other);
    }

    // Ordered by the fields in turn, which is how a tuple of the same values orders
    bool operator<(const NamesStruct& other) const {
      if (this->other < other.other)
        return true;
      if (other.other < this->other)
        return false;
      if (this->result < other.result)
        return true;
      if (other.result < this->result)
        return false;
      if (this->java < other.java)
        return true;
      if (other.java < this->java)
        return false;
      if (this->krpc < other.krpc)
        return true;
      if (other.krpc < this->krpc)
        return false;
      if (this->class_ < other.class_)
        return true;
      if (other.class_ < this->class_)
        return false;
      if (this->bound < other.bound)
        return true;
      return false;
    }

    bool operator>(const NamesStruct& other) const {
      return other < *this;
    }

    bool operator<=(const NamesStruct& other) const {
      return !(other < *this);
    }

    bool operator>=(const NamesStruct& other) const {
      return !(*this < other);
    }
  };

}  // namespace services

namespace encoder {

  inline std::string encode(const services::TestService::DeprecatedEnum& value) {
    return krpc::encoder::encode(static_cast<int32_t>(value));
  }

  inline std::string encode(const services::TestService::NamesEnum& value) {
    return krpc::encoder::encode(static_cast<int32_t>(value));
  }

  inline std::string encode(const services::TestService::PartlyDeprecatedEnum& value) {
    return krpc::encoder::encode(static_cast<int32_t>(value));
  }

  inline std::string encode(const services::TestService::TestEnum& value) {
    return krpc::encoder::encode(static_cast<int32_t>(value));
  }

  inline std::string encode(const services::TestService::TestFlags& value) {
    return krpc::encoder::encode(static_cast<int32_t>(value));
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::TestService::DeprecatedStruct& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.value));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.old_value));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::TestService::NamesStruct& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.other));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.result));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.java));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.krpc));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.class_));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.bound));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::TestService::PartlyDeprecatedStruct& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.value));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.old_value));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::TestService::TestFlagsStruct& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.flags_field));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::TestService::TestNestedNullableStruct& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.list_field));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.string_field));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::TestService::TestStruct& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.int_field));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.string_field));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.enum_field));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.list_field));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::TestService::TestNestedStruct& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.struct_field));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.object_field));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.string_field));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

  // A structure is sent as the values of its fields in order, which is the same encoding as
  // a tuple of those values. See the note on the collection encoders for why the message it
  // is sent in is kept between calls.
  inline std::string encode(const services::TestService::TestNullableStruct& value) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    structMessage.Clear();
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.int_field));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.nullable_int_field));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.nullable_enum_field));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.nullable_string_field));
    structMessage.add_items(encode_item(value.nullable_object_field));
    return krpc::encoder::encode(structMessage);
  }

}  // namespace encoder

namespace decoder {

  inline void decode(services::TestService::DeprecatedEnum& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    int32_t x;
    decode(x, data, connection);
    value = static_cast<services::TestService::DeprecatedEnum>(x);
  }

  inline void decode(services::TestService::NamesEnum& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    int32_t x;
    decode(x, data, connection);
    value = static_cast<services::TestService::NamesEnum>(x);
  }

  inline void decode(services::TestService::PartlyDeprecatedEnum& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    int32_t x;
    decode(x, data, connection);
    value = static_cast<services::TestService::PartlyDeprecatedEnum>(x);
  }

  inline void decode(services::TestService::TestEnum& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    int32_t x;
    decode(x, data, connection);
    value = static_cast<services::TestService::TestEnum>(x);
  }

  inline void decode(services::TestService::TestFlags& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    int32_t x;
    decode(x, data, connection);
    value = static_cast<services::TestService::TestFlags>(x);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::TestService::DeprecatedStruct& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 2)
      throw EncodingError("Value for DeprecatedStruct does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.value, structMessage.items(0), connection);
    decode_item(value.old_value, structMessage.items(1), connection);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::TestService::NamesStruct& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 6)
      throw EncodingError("Value for NamesStruct does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.other, structMessage.items(0), connection);
    decode_item(value.result, structMessage.items(1), connection);
    decode_item(value.java, structMessage.items(2), connection);
    decode_item(value.krpc, structMessage.items(3), connection);
    decode_item(value.class_, structMessage.items(4), connection);
    decode_item(value.bound, structMessage.items(5), connection);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::TestService::PartlyDeprecatedStruct& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 2)
      throw EncodingError("Value for PartlyDeprecatedStruct does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.value, structMessage.items(0), connection);
    decode_item(value.old_value, structMessage.items(1), connection);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::TestService::TestFlagsStruct& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 1)
      throw EncodingError("Value for TestFlagsStruct does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.flags_field, structMessage.items(0), connection);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::TestService::TestNestedNullableStruct& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 2)
      throw EncodingError("Value for TestNestedNullableStruct does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.list_field, structMessage.items(0), connection);
    decode_item(value.string_field, structMessage.items(1), connection);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::TestService::TestStruct& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 4)
      throw EncodingError("Value for TestStruct does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.int_field, structMessage.items(0), connection);
    decode_item(value.string_field, structMessage.items(1), connection);
    decode_item(value.enum_field, structMessage.items(2), connection);
    decode_item(value.list_field, structMessage.items(3), connection);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::TestService::TestNestedStruct& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 3)
      throw EncodingError("Value for TestNestedStruct does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.struct_field, structMessage.items(0), connection);
    decode_item(value.object_field, structMessage.items(1), connection);
    decode_item(value.string_field, structMessage.items(2), connection);
  }

  // Fields are only ever appended to a structure, so a value from a newer server may carry
  // more items than this client knows about, and those are ignored.
  inline void decode(services::TestService::TestNullableStruct& value, const std::string& data, ::krpc::Connection* connection) {
    static thread_local krpc::schema::Tuple structMessage;
    if (!structMessage.ParseFromString(data)) throw EncodingError("Failed to decode message");
    if (structMessage.items_size() < 5)
      throw EncodingError("Value for TestNullableStruct does not have all of its fields");
    decode_item(value.int_field, structMessage.items(0), connection);
    decode_item(value.nullable_int_field, structMessage.items(1), connection);
    decode_item(value.nullable_enum_field, structMessage.items(2), connection);
    decode_item(value.nullable_string_field, structMessage.items(3), connection);
    decode_item(value.nullable_object_field, structMessage.items(4), connection);
  }

}  // namespace decoder

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::TestService::DeprecatedStruct& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.value));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.old_value));
  return seed;
}

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::TestService::NamesStruct& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.other));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.result));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.java));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.krpc));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.class_));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.bound));
  return seed;
}

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::TestService::PartlyDeprecatedStruct& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.value));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.old_value));
  return seed;
}

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::TestService::TestFlagsStruct& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.flags_field));
  return seed;
}

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::TestService::TestNestedNullableStruct& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.list_field));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.string_field));
  return seed;
}

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::TestService::TestStruct& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.int_field));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.string_field));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.enum_field));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.list_field));
  return seed;
}

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::TestService::TestNestedStruct& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.struct_field));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.object_field));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.string_field));
  return seed;
}

inline std::size_t hash_value(const services::TestService::TestNullableStruct& value) {
  std::size_t seed = 0;
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.int_field));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.nullable_int_field));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.nullable_enum_field));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.nullable_string_field));
  hash_combine(&seed, hash_value(value.nullable_object_field));
  return seed;
}

// A collection encoder or decoder calls encode and decode on the type it contains
// without qualifying them. The ones above are declared after those templates, so
// argument dependent lookup is what reaches them. It searches the namespace that
// encloses the type, which is this one. These name the same functions here, as a
// second function with the same signature is ambiguous to a compiler that finds both.
namespace services {
  using ::krpc::encoder::encode;
  using ::krpc::decoder::decode;
  using ::krpc::hash_value;
}  // namespace services

namespace services {

inline TestService::TestService(const ::krpc::Connection& connection):
  ::krpc::RemoteService(connection) {
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "TestService", "CustomException",
    [] (const ::std::string& msg) { throw CustomException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "TestService", "DeprecatedException",
    [] (const ::std::string& msg) { throw DeprecatedException(msg); });
  _connection.add_exception_thrower(
    "TestService", "RuntimeError",
    [] (const ::std::string& msg) { throw RuntimeError(msg); });
}

inline ::std::string TestService::add_multiple_values(float x, ::std::int32_t y, ::std::int64_t z) {
  ::std::vector<::krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(3);
  _args.push_back(::krpc::encoder::encode_arg(x));
  _args.push_back(::krpc::encoder::encode_arg(y));
  _args.push_back(::krpc::encoder::encode_arg(z));
  auto _data = this->_connection.invoke("TestService", "AddMultipleValues", _args, ::krpc::Connection::Returns::value);
  ::std::string _result{};
  ::krpc::decoder::decode_result(_result, _data, &this->_connection);
  return _result;
}

inline ::std::map<::std::string, ::krpc::services::TestService::TestClass> TestService::add_to_object_dictionary(::std::map<::std::string, ::krpc::services::TestService::TestClass> d, ::std::string key) {
  ::std::vector<::krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(2);
  _args.push_back(::krpc::encoder::encode_arg(d));
  _args.push_back(::krpc::encoder::encode_arg(key));
  auto _data = this->_connection.invoke("TestService", "AddToObjectDictionary", _args, ::krpc::Connection::Returns::value);
  ::std::map<::std::string, ::krpc::services::TestService::TestClass> _result{};
  ::krpc::decoder::decode_result(_result, _data, &this->_connection);
  return _result;
}

inline ::std::vector<double> TestService::double_special_defaults(double nan = ::std::numeric_limits<double>::quiet_NaN(), double infinity = ::std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity(), double negative_infinity = -::std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity(), double maximum = (::std::numeric_limits<double>::max)(), double lowest = ::std::numeric_limits<double>::lowest()) {
  ::std::vector<::krpc::encoder::Value> _args;
  _args.reserve(5);
  _args.push_back(::krpc::encoder::encode_arg(nan));
  _args.push_back(::krpc::encoder::encode_arg(infinity));
  _args.push_back(::krpc::encoder::encode_arg(negative_infinity));
  _args.push_back(::krpc::encoder::encode_arg(maximum));
  _args.push_back(::krpc::encoder::encode_arg(lowest));
  auto _data = this->_connection.invoke("TestService", "DoubleSpecialDefaults", _args, ::krpc::Connection::Returns::value);
  ::std::vector<double> _result{};
  ::krpc::decoder::decode_result(_result, _data, &this->_connection);
  return _result;
}

inline ::std::string TestService::double_to_string(double value) {
  ::std::vector<::krpc::encoder::Value> _args;

// and an unordered container of one needs nothing further.

    return krpc::hash_value(value);
